115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

1. 第 3 季出口較上年同季增 755.9 億美元(+44.6%)，以資通與視聽產
品增 367.6 億美元(+55.0%)、電子零組件增 308.4 億美元(+51.2%)
增加較多。 
2. 累計 1 至 9 月出口較上年同期增 2,089.8 億美元(+46.2%)，其中資
通與視聽產品增 1,222.2 億美元(+72.4%)、電子零組件增 726.6 億
美元(+45.7%)。

等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月對東協出口創新高，對美國、陸港為單月次高 
1. 對美國出口 274.6 億美元，較上年同月增 141.5 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 124.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)、 電子零組件增10.1
億美元(+1.3 倍)、機械增 2.2 億美元(+42.3%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 206.2 億美元，較上年同月增 47.8 億美元
(+30.2%)，其中電子零組件增 34.9 億美元(+30.5%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.7 億美元(+39.0%)、機械增 1.9 億美元(+43.7%)。 
3. 對東協出口 188.8 億美元，較上年同月增 91.2 億美元(+93.4%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 49.7 億美元(+2.2 倍)、電子零組件增 32.0
億美元(+63.3%)、礦產品增 4.8 億美元(+1.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 45.3 億美元，較上年同月增 7.7 億美元(+20.6%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 5.7 億美元(+34.1%)、電子零組件增 2.2 億美
元(+56.6%)，基本金屬及其製品減 0.8 億美元(-20.5%)。 
5. 對日本出口 30.7 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+13.4%)，其
中電子零組件增 1.5 億美元(+11.0%)、基本金屬及其製品增 0.6 億
美元(+32.5%)、機械增 0.5 億美元(+29.1%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月對美國出口 2,077.8 億美元，較上年同期增 773.1 億美
元(+59.3%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,600.9 億美元，增 355.3 億美
元(+28.5%)， 對東協出口1,339.6 億美元 ， 增463.6 億美元(+52.9%)，
對歐洲出口 421.8 億美元 ， 增138.4 億美元(+48.8%)， 對日本出口277.7
億美元，增 58.3 億美元(+26.6%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月自中國大陸與香港進口 135.0 億美元 ， 較上年同月增50.1 億美元
(+59.1%)，自南韓進口 116.3 億美元，增52.6 億美元(+82.5%)，自東
協進口 106.0 億美元，增 52.6 億美元(+98.4%)，自日本進口 64.3 億
美元，增 17.7 億美元(+38.0%)，自歐洲進口 50.2 億美元，增 3.5 億
美元(+7.4%)，自美國進口 48.1 億美元，增 6.5 億美元(+15.5%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增77.6%、
自南韓增 65.8%、自中國大陸與香港增 42.8%、自日本增 25.7%、自
美國增 21.4%、自歐洲增 10.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）主要國家/地區今年 1-8 月外貿景氣普遍走升，以南韓(1-9 月)出口年
增 56.8%最為強勁，香港、新加坡亦分別年增 49.0%、39.8%；中國
大陸、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-7 月)、日本各增
9.7%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-8 月以香港進口年增 50.5%較
突出，新加坡、中國大陸分別年增 37.3%、27.0%，南韓(1-9 月)、德
國(1-7 月)分別增 19.7%、10.0%，日本、美國各增 7.8%、4.2%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

南亞(1303)：電子材料獲利與業外轉投資貢獻優於預期 
建議：維持買進

一、3Q26 自結獲利優於預期 
3Q26 營收季增 14.3%至 955.97 億元， 加上電子材料獲
利大幅成長，以及業外南亞科及台塑化挹注，單季自結
EPS 達 4.90 元、優於預期；累計前三季 EPS 為 10.07
元。各部門營益率中，電子材料自 2Q26 的 19.9%跳升
至 30.9%，塑膠加工則為 5.4%、化工部門 0.6%、聚酯
部門 0.2%。 化工與聚酯部門獲利衰退主要是2Q26 有低
價庫存，塑膠加工則因通膨干擾終端消費。然而，EG
在中國華東港口庫存嚴重偏低下，近一季報價及利差表
現好轉，抵銷可塑劑產品與 BPA 的虧損，聚酯部門則有
美國外銷動能挹注，支撐兩個部門 3Q26 小幅獲利。展
望 4Q26， 終端消費依舊沒有明顯的動能 ， 但EG 報價及
利差持穩且聚酯朝電子應用發展，管理層預期化工、聚
酯部門營益率與 3Q26 相當，電子材料在漲價延續及產
品組合優化下，營利率有望持續走升。

二、2027 年營運動能依舊為電子材料與南亞科貢獻 
3Q26 電子材料營收達 555 億元，其中，CCL 營收比重
達 31%、PCB(南電) 29%， 銅箔與Epoxy 則各佔 11%、
玻纖 7%。 獲利結構來看 ， 玻纖營益率走升至40%以上，
CCL 則季增 10 個百分點至 30%並高於 PCB 的 26%，
銅箔受惠加工費調漲達 15%，Epoxy 則維持 10~11%左
右。2027 年電子材料依舊是成長主力，部門營收比重將
達 60%以上，營益率有望在單季接近 40%的水準，但材
料漲價趨勢會從玻纖轉為 CCL。轉投資方面，南亞科因
記憶體市場供需缺口急遽放大 ， 預估將貢獻2026、2027
年稅前 EPS 9.17 與 16.74 元。

三、高階 CCL 訂單自 2H26 逐步出貨，2027 年效益將
明顯放大 
2025 年南亞 CCL 單價約每張 500 元，3Q26 已走升至
1,000~1,100 元/張，主要受惠漲價與產品組合優化。管
理層預期 4Q26 維持每月漲福 5~10%，且有機會延續至
1Q27。3Q26 中下旬 M 系列在 CCL 的營收比重由 20%
提升至 30%，漲價及產品組合優化都有影響，1Q27 在
M6、M7 訂單放量下會有更明顯的增長。目前 M4 CCL
單價約 1,100~1,200 元/張，M6 約當每張 2,200~2,300
元，M7 單價則跳升至 4,000 元以上 ， 有助2027 年 ASP
漲幅進一步擴大。此外，3Q26 的 CCL 產能利用率達
90~95%，現階段 CCL 月產能 618 萬張，規劃樹林、新
港將進行產線整改，預計 2026 年底跟明年可以各增加
約 15 萬張/月。 
四、維持買進理由 
3Q26 自結獲利優於預期，電子材料漲價與 CCL 產品組
合優化支撐本業中長期動能，轉投資南亞科(2408)與台
塑化(6505)大幅挹注業外收益 ， 上修2026、2027 年 EPS
分別為 15.72 及 29.24 元。考量獲利顯著成長有助股息
提高，且市場因公司獲利結構轉型而 Re-rating，維持買
進評等，目標價調升至 450 元(20x 2027 本業 EPS+10x 
2027 南亞科 EPS)。

Document 031: 20261027【投研報告】市場快訊_MSCI降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待

MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market） 」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定 。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至 「邊境市場 （Frontier Market
） 」 ，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低
觸及 4,907.37 點 。 雖然20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈 ， 但26 日再度下跌 1.22%

，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」， 原則上將不再納入MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
億美元被動資金流出，其中 UBS 估計約 40 億美元，並指出主動型基金另有約 150 億
美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外 。 相關改革有助改

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續 ， 以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證 ， 我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異 ， 區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響 ， 以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

顧
歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。

市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。

資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。

• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續

• 台股第四季有望先蹲後跳： 10月初以來，台股在盤中觸及歷史高點 49,968點後面臨一波修正 ，主要反映在新一輪的財報公
布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，
台股維持區間震盪的可能性較高；中期來看，美國期中選後往往因政治不確定性下降，歷史統計股市上漲機率高，加上台灣

年底集團作帳及選舉行情，正向看待年底台股表現。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本

非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

Document 113: 261020_daiwa_ZDT

Source PDF: all report pdf/261020_daiwa_ZDT.pdf

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT), the world’s largest PCB 
manufacturer by revenue, generated revenue of TWD182.5bn in 2025. 
Historically focused on consumer electronics and smartphones, the 
company is shifting towards higher-value AI-server PCBs (HLC/iHDI), 
optical-module PCBs (mSAP), and IC substrates (ABF/BT). These 
businesses accounted for 21.6% of its revenue in 1H26, up from 11.7% in 
2025. Management targets this ratio to reach c.25% in 2026 and c.30% in 
2027. To support this shift, ZDT has 22 fabs under construction and plans 
to build nine more, primarily for mSAP/HDI, HLC, and ABF substrates.

Highlights: ZDT’s “One ZDT strategy” is gradually bearing fruit. Since 
2018, management has pursued its “One ZDT strategy” to broaden the 
product portfolio – including HLC, substrates, mSAP/HDI, rigid-flex and 
SLP – by leveraging its strengths in FPCB and consumer HDI. This 
strategy is beginning to pay off, and management expects its fast-growing 
segments, including mSAP HDI for optical transceivers, AI-server HDI and 
ABF/BT substrates, to gain momentum from this year. Management 
expects revenue from these segments to increase by c.150% YoY and 
account for c.25% of total revenue (or c.TWD50bn) in 2026, and to further 
increase by 100% YoY to TWD100bn in 2027. ZDT targets these fast-
growing segments to reach 30% of its revenue mix in 2027 and c.45-50% 
in 2030. Given current progress, the 2030 target could be reached in 2028. 
For mSAP HDI, management expects the 800G/1.6T transceiver product 
mix to shift to 7:3 in 2027, from 5:5 in 2026, and aims to become the largest 
supplier of optical-transceiver HDI in 2027. In AI-server HDI, we expect the 
2 leading ASIC vendors to gradually shift from pure HLC to thick HDI 
boards – a combination of HLC and HDI – in their next-generation designs, 
with ZDT a potential beneficiary. This could drive c.3x growth in ZDT’s 
business in 2027.

Product mix to support gross margin expansion. Margin expansion is 
increasingly driven by a favourable product mix. Gross margin reached 
22.4% in 1H26 (vs. 16.5% in 1H25). Management indicated that optical-
transceiver HDIs currently have the highest gross margin, at over 40%, 
followed by IC substrates, with ABF margins potentially reaching 50-60% 
and BT margins around 30% as a long-term target. Server HLC margins 
are around 20-30%, while HDI margins should be higher given their greater 
process complexity. With margins in traditional businesses at c.20%, ZDT 
expects growth in the newer segments to drive further margin expansion.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 36/19x PER for 2026/27E based on 
Bloomberg consensus. It’s past 3-year PER range is 10-30x.

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Genius Electronic Optical
Core resilience with CPO upside optionality
Raise PT to NT$1,250 on potential CPO contribution and intact core business
We reiterate Buy and raise our  price target to NT$1,250 from NT$700, rolling valuation
to 2028E and moving from P/B to a P/E-based SOTP that separately values the core lens
business and the emerging co-packaged optics (CPO) business. We believe Genius is
making progress sampling three precision components inside the fiber array unit (FAU)

targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.  We see potential for the company
to qualify the leading FAU module supplier into NVIDIA's CPO program. At the same
time, its core business is outperforming Largan, growing +1% YoY in Q326 vs. -11%
YoY for Largan as its share rises on periscope lens. Compared with the pressure on
Largan's margins from green electricity prices, we believe Genius should maintain its
margin, as its main production site is in Xiamen, whereas Largan's production in Taiwan

is affected by higher electricity prices.
Core intact: periscope share gains offset muted smartphone demand
September sales of NT$2.67bn (−2% MoM, +1% YoY) took Q326 to NT$7.82bn (+38%
QoQ, +1% YoY), in-line with VA consensus. Genius grew 10% YoY in 9M26 against 2%
at Largan, which we attribute to share gains in periscope lenses for premium iPhones,
offsetting legacy price cuts and lower VR lens shipments. We still keep 7% below VA on
4Q26E revenue (NT$7.34bn, −6% QoQ) reflecting muted iPhone sales and no content

in the iPhone 18 Pro's new variable-aperture lens.
CPO component strategy offers a different route to market from Largan
Unlike Largan's pursuit of complete FAU assembly, Genius is targeting high precision
optical components within the module, including: 1) high-precision prism to collimate
light from the PIC into the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place,
and 3) MT ferrules (high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). We

believe Genius' customer is one of the key module suppliers on NVIDIA's 2H27 program
targeting over 50% share. Our base case models a complete-FAU market of US$1.85bn
in 2028E and US$4.45bn in 2030E, with Genius base case for US$185m and US$668m
representing 10% and 15% revenue share of the complete-FAU revenue TAM.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with  PT lifted to NT$1,250
We fine-tune our 2026/2027 basic EPS estimates from NT$37.2/NT$41.5 to NT$36.7/NT

$44.0 and introduce 2028E EPS of NT$49.25. We move from P/B to P/E SOTP as the core
business now delivers steadier earnings and CPO warrants separate valuation. Our NT
$1,250 PT comprises NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) and NT$505
for CPO (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E). Catalysts include qualifications at
leading FAU makers and the board approved 7mn share private private placement with
potential strategic investor (albeit 6% dilution if issued). Our US$1.8bn CPO value

compares to US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.

### Page 2

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Genius' CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
Yes, from H227E into 2028E. We see Genius' CPO strategy as feasible and lower-risk than Largan's.
Largan's goal is to supply the complete FAU; Genius is entering at the component level, supplying V-
grooves, MT ferrules and prisms to FAU makers. This lets Genius sell into multiple FAU designs and

integrators, limiting its downside if the market converges on a single winning architecture.
Management believes multi-sourcing is inevitable once volumes scale and has seen early indications
of potentially substantial demand from US optical players, including Corning and Coherent. Genius
has a partnership with FOCI and is pursuing other FAU integrators including we believe Coherent;
management suggested further updates could come in the near term. Some parts already ship in

small volumes, but we do not expect meaningful revenue until H227 or 2028.
Q: Will Genius benefit from further lens technology upgrades in the coming years?
Yes. We expect premium lens participation and product mix to support core revenue growth of 8.0%,
9.7% and 9.1% in 2026-28E. We believe Genius is gaining share from Largan in Apple's periscope
lens supply chain after initially lagging. Apple (70–80% of Genius' sales) sell-through units grew 9%

YoY YTD through August according to Counterpoint, and Genius' 9M26 revenue grew 10% YoY on
a similar trajectory. Largan grew only 2% in 9M26, with Q326 down 11% YoY. For the iPhone 18
series, Genius participates in every lens spec except the variable-aperture upgrade, where it stepped
back because of pricing pressure from Chinese competitors. Management says Genius has the
technical capability and could enter if future specs or design changes improve the opportunity.

Genius offers a combination of an established earnings base from smartphone lens and potential
exposure to the passive components required for optical interconnects. We remain constructive on
the lens business and see CPO as a potentially material addition from 2028. The component strategy
provides several routes to customers, but the valuation should reflect uncertainty over qualified
content, allocation and margins. Our Buy rating and NT$1,250 PT is based  on NT$745 base business

(15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505 for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in
2028E). The CPO option for Genius is US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.
Genius share gains are showing in reported sales, with YTD sales +10% YoY vs. +2% YoY for Largan.
The company is also participating in every spec except variable aperture.  For CPO, Genius delivered
samples of its prism, V-groove, and MT ferrule to FOCI at the end of May, with initial feedback broadly

on track and no major issues reported to date. Our checks indicate the company is also positioning to
supply one of the leading FAU module suppliers for NVIDIA's CPO program from H227.
At NT$1,060, the shares trade at 24.1x our 2027E core EPS and 21.5x our 2028E core EPS, factoring
some of the CPO opportunity in the multiple rather than in estimates relative to the 10 year average
17x P/E. Applying a 15x valuation to the smartphone lens business + 40x to the CPO option from our

scenario of NT$9.30 EPS in 2028E yields upside from the last close at NT$1,060 to a NT$1,250 share
price.

With CPO revenue contribution <5% in 2026 per management guidance, Genius' lens

### Page 4

Genius position in the CPO value chain
Genius is targeting the CPO market as a component supplier while Largan is advancing
from components into full-FAU assembly. Genius is sampling three components for CPO
with FAU makers, including: 1) high-precision prism to redirect light from the PIC into
the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place, and 3) MT ferrules
(high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). The companies overlap in

capability, with Largan also having PMLA and fiber arrays but not getting into some
components like MT ferrules and V-Grooves.
Genius's optical design, precision tooling, metrology and volume-manufacturing
experience offer it a good entry point for this market. Commercial differentiation will
depend on reproducible optical performance, dimensional tolerances, thermal stability
and customer yield.  It has announced collaboration with FOCI and  we believe has

potential to also work with one of NVIDIA's leading module suppliers for its H227
program.

Source: Genius
Annual FAU TAM reaches US$1.85bn in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E in

our base case
We update our FAU (fiber array unit) TAM factoring CPO/NPO penetration into
AI networking switches, NVIDIA multi-rack systems, scale-up on compute and switch
boards, and non-NVIDIA accelerators.  We assume one passive FAU assembly connecting
a fiber array to a photonic engine with one unit per optical engine.
We assume one FAU per optical engine and 32 per switch ASIC in our base case,
matching NVIDIA's 32x3.2T engines on its 102.4T Spectrum-X package. Broadcom's

102.4Tb/s Davisson uses 16 engines at 6.4Tb/s, so our bear case assumes an even mix of
these two designs (24 per ASIC). We note that higher switch bandwidth does not
automatically increase FAU count if engine bandwidth rises at the same rate. Our 2030E
assumption maintains the 32 FAUs per switch based on bandwidth rising to 204.8Tb/s

Our base case for the FAU market sums up 4 opportunities for CPO/NPO attach:
1. Scale-out and scale-across switches
We estimate annual switch ASIC shipments x CPO + NPO penetration x optical engines
per ASIC x FAUs per engine. In 2028E, 25% CPO/NPO penetration or 1.0m 102.4T
switch ASICs at 32 FAUs each produces 8.0m FAUs. In 2030E, 55% of 1.1mn

204.8T switch ASICs at 32 FAUs drives a market size of 19.4mn FAUs.

2. NVIDIA's multi-rack connections for Rubin Ultra and Feynman
NVIDIA at its 2026 GTC San Jose proposed a multi-rack option to network together 8
Oberon NVL72 racks for the Rubin Ultra generation and 8 Kyber NVL144 racks.

Feedback at the time showed  possible transceiver or NPO connections for Rubin Ultra
and CPO connections for Feynman.  Our 2028 base case uses 110,000 racks, 15% multi-
rack adoption and 75% CPO/NPO penetration, giving approximately 12,400 connected
racks.
For 2030E, our rack count falls to 35,000 racks as we assume Kyber would have 4x the
number of GPUs, equivalent to 140k Oberon equivalents. We assume the 35,000 racks
at 55% multi-rack adoption x 80% CPO/NPO attach gives 15,400 connected racks. We

assume about 216 and 576 inter-rack FAUs per rack, respectively, driving a market
opportunity of 2.7mn FAUs in 2028E and 8.9mn in 2030E.

Overall market opportunity: 12.3mn/35.6mn generates a US$1.85bn/US
$4.5bn TAM in 2028/2030E
We sum four pools: scale-out/scale-across switches, NVIDIA multi-rack pods, CPO native
NVIDIA GPUs and non-NVIDIA scale-up.  We add 10%/5% for FAUs lost at downstream
attach and test and for field spares, driving a market size of in our base case of 12.3mn
FAUs in 2028E and 35.6mn in 2030E. At US$150/US$125 per FAU, that is a US$1.85bn

market in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E (bear/bull US$0.5bn/US$3.5bn in 2028E; US
$1.4bn/US$9.0bn in 2030E). The expansion to 2030E comes from wider optical
adoption and greater content in scale-up systems, despite an assumed FAU ASP decline
from US$150 to US$125.

Scenario analysis on Genius CPO opportunity

### Page 7

We assume in base case Genius captures 10% of the TAM in 2028E and 15% by 2030E.
The base case in 2028E is consistent with US$30 of prism, V-groove and ferrule content
in half of all FAUs or US$15 average content spread across the FAU market.  The
2030E bull case (25%) or US$37.50 would require near full content of some of the sub-
components or otherwise ability to integrate more of the solution value by that time.
Our bear case assumes only 1% share of the opportunity in 2028E and 5% of the

opportunity in 2030E, with EPS even by 2030E falling to NT$4 and yielding limited
valuation potential for the shares.

Reiterate Buy and raise PT to NT$1,250 valuing in CPO optionality

We adjust our valuation method from P/B to P/E SOTP to better reflect the company’s
core business and its opportunity within CPO industry while the core smartphone
business is demonstrating more stable earnings to use P/E vs. prior P/B. Our PT at NT
$1,250 is based on NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505
for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E) with catalysts from
upcoming qualifications into leading FAU makers and potential strategic private

placement with board approving 7mn share private placement (albeit 6% dilution if
issued). We note Genius CPO option valued at US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our
Largan valuation.
Our price target uses 2028 earnings for both businesses, reflecting the modeled start of
meaningful CPO contribution. We apply 15x core EPS of NT$49.25 and 40x CPO EPS of
NT$12.72 for a NT$1,250 price target. The 40x multiple assumes the optical business
can sustain strong growth after its initial ramp; qualification, concentration and margin

risk limit the confidence that can be placed on that earnings stream.
In the upside case, stronger premium lens participation and manufacturing execution lift
2028E core EPS to NT$54.32. Faster optical adoption expands the 2028E FAU market to
US$3.46bn; 15% effective revenue capture and a 31% pretax margin produce
approximately NT$36.88 CPO EPS. Applying 15x to core earnings and 42x to CPO
earnings gives approximately NT$2,365.
In the downside case, weaker lens demand and margin pressure lower core EPS to NT

$44.29. Slower optical adoption leaves a US$0.46bn FAU market, of which Genius
captures 1%, producing only NT$0.29 CPO EPS. Applying 12x and 38x gives
approximately NT$540. The downside is driven by both a weaker core business and a
much smaller optical contribution.

Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Genius (3406.TW) trading at 1,060 at closed Oct 8.
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE (NT$2,365): Lens upgrades accelerate and Genius gains share in higher-end
specs. 2027E/28E sales grow 11.8%/9.1% YoY (base 9.7%/9.1%), and GM expands to
35.6%/35.5% (base 34.4%/34.3%) on better yields and mix. 2028E core EPS of NT
$54.3 (+10% vs base) at 15x gives NT$815. Faster CPO adoption and stronger AI capex

lift the 2028E FAU TAM to US$3.5bn (19.8mn units at US$175); at 15% capture, CPO
sales reach US$519mn and CPO EPS NT$36.9, worth NT$1,550 at 42x.
BASE (NT$1,250):  Core NT$745 (15x NT$49.25) plus CPO NT$505 (40x NT$12.72), as
above.
DOWNSIDE (NT540): Smartphone demand weakens, legacy price erosion continues
and share gains stall. 2027E sales grow 7.4% YoY with GM of 33.4%, and 2028E core
EPS falls to NT$44.3 (−10% vs base), worth NT$530 at 12x. CPO adoption is slow

(10%/3% CPO/NPO in scale-out, no ASIC attach), leaving a US$0.46bn TAM; at 1%
capture, CPO is worth NT$11.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our Genius price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model. Genius' growth is
highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by volatility in handset
demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their camera design.

Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of new
technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business. We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from

smartphone OEM clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a
slower-thanexpected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success
below the market's elevated expectations. We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-
expected CPO qualification process at Largan. In addition, if the company demonstrates
exceptionally efficient and highly accurate automation capabilities, it could support greater
market share gains within the industry than we currently anticipate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

旺矽 (6223 TT)  Buy

旺矽營收優於預期 ，CPO 設備出貨將帶動評價提升 ， 給Buy
的投資建議。  
投資建議 ：旺矽營收優於預期， CPO 設備出貨將帶動評價提升，
給 Buy 的投資建議。  
09/2026 營收優於群益預期 ：旺矽 09/2026 營收為 20.48 億元，
MoM-0.02% 、 YoY+82.23% ，累計營收為 150.68 億元，
YoY+58.02% 。實際月營收與群益預估的 16.50 億元，差異為
19.43% ，優於預期。  
3Q26 優於市場預期 ：市場先前預期旺矽 3Q26 營收 QoQ+0~5% ，
實際營收 QoQ+12.76% ，優於預期。  
旺矽有佈局 CPO 測試設備 ：旺矽的 Insertion 3 設備預計 4Q26
向晶圓代工 、IC 設計 、OSAT 送樣 ，1H27 有望進入量產 ；Insertion 
2 設備預計 2H26 會有較明顯的結果。  
旺矽積極擴產： 旺矽表示，目前在許多客戶端都是第一供應商，
供貨比重大多為 80% ，但之前因為產能供不應求，有外溢到第
二供應商，現在有積極擴產。  
旺矽預計 2027 年提高 PCB 的自製率： 探針卡可分為測試頭、
載板及 PCB 。旺矽表示，探針及載板均已全數自製， PCB 預計
2027 年開始量產，目標將 PCB 的自製率提升至 50% 。  
群益預估旺矽 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 64.86 元 /110.49 元： 群
益預估旺矽 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 214.11 億元 /363.85 億元，
YoY+60.13%/+69.94% ；稅後純益 63.57 億元 /108.28 億元，
YoY+100.11%/+70.35% ；稅後 EPS 64.86 元 /110.49 元。

準，以健全公司治理。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

SK하이닉스 (000660)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 3,200,000원 
현재주가 (10.8) 1,681,000원 
상승여력 90.4%

Document 203: AI泡沫承压：这是大甩卖的终点，还是下一个买入时机？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI泡沫承压：这是大甩卖的终点，还是下一个买入时机？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

全球科技股遭遇⼤规模抛售，⾼盛数据显⽰，过去两周净卖出约 200 亿美元，科技股波动率
溢价升⾄五年区间第 98 百分位。市场聚焦科技巨头财报，检验 AI 投资回报与融资可持续
性。摩根⼤通认为， 11 ⽉初或现买⼊窗⼝，但仍需等待仓位出清、盈利验证及信⽤利差收
窄。
摘要：
科技板块正承受多重压⼒，对冲基⾦⼤举削减敞⼝，波动率警⽰信号频闪，与此同时， AI 融资成
本攀升、信⽤⻛险交织蔓延，令这轮 AI 热潮⾯临⽇益严峻的资⾦考验。
过去两周，据⾼盛数据，全球科技股遭到约 200 亿美元的净卖出，抛售强度已触及 -3.3 个标准差
的极端⽔平。纳斯达克 100 指数（ NDX ）⽬前仍困于⾃ 5 ⽉以来的震荡区间， 29000 点的期货关
⼝与其下⽅约 28500 点的 200 ⽇均线，构成当前最关键的技术⽀撑带。
摩根⼤通⽅⾯，其分析师 Privorotsky 指出，尽管 AI 需求依然强劲，但融资环境正在收紧 ——年
内 AI 基础设施债务融资规模已逾 5000 亿美元，超⼤规模云服务商的融资成本较此前上升约 120 个

基点。英伟达与其 GPU 客⼾之间的融资关联，正被视为潜在的信⽤⻛险传导链条，⼀旦 GPU 运
营商资⾦链趋紧，压⼒或将反馈⾄上游供应商。
仓位清洗：与 7 ⽉惊⼈相似
本轮科技股抛售的规模与烈度，令市场联想起今年 7 ⽉的那次回调。
⾼盛数据显⽰，过去两周 TMT 板块累计卖出约 200 亿美元，已接近 7 ⽉抛售期间的 230 亿美元。
受冲击最重的交易品种，价格⾛势⼏乎如出⼀辙。不同之处在于，标普 500 指数本轮仅下跌约
2.4% ，低于 7 ⽉ 3.4% 的跌幅，指数层⾯的波动相对收敛。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

与此同时，⾼盛⻛险偏好指标录得有记录以来最⼤单次跌幅之⼀，跌⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来的最低⽔
平。 TMT 板块基本⾯净敞⼝降⾄年内低点，尽管总敞⼝仍处于第 63 百分位 —— 这意味着净仓位
虽已压缩，但总杠杆仍是当前潜在痛点所在。
科技恐慌溢价：⾼达 98 百分位
恐慌情绪并未均匀分布于整个市场，⽽是⾼度集中在科技板块。
⾼盛分析显⽰， NDX 与 SPX 之间的波动率利差已急速扩张⾄五年区间的第 98 百分位，上周四单
⽇扩张幅度更创五年来第⼆⼤纪录。科技板块专属的波动率定价相对于⼤盘整体波动已处于异
常昂贵的⽔平，由此催⽣出潜在的相对价值交易机会。 AI 相关股票之间的联动性显著增强，但指
数层⾯的波动率整体仍保持相对克制。

### Page 3

AI 融资测试：超⼤规模云⼚商成关键变量
本周，⾕歌、微软、亚⻢逊的财报密集披露，市场关注的焦点已超越资本开⽀指引本⾝。
据摩根⼤通，年内 AI 基础设施债务吸收规模已超 5000 亿美元，超⼤规模云服务商融资成本上升
约 120 个基点。在此背景下，市场真正在意的问题是：积压订单能否有效转化为营收，以及下⼀
轮投资扩张将如何取得融资。
值得注意的是， AI 相关信⽤违约互换（ CDS ）利差近期明显⾛阔，但摩根⼤通认为，这在很⼤
程度上并⾮反映基本⾯恶化，⽽是银⾏机构为对冲⾃⾝持续扩张的数据中⼼敞⼝⽽主动买⼊保
护的结果。 CDS 与现⾦债券之间的利差扩⼤，或正在形成新的相对价值机会。
英伟达⽣态系统的信⽤传导⻛险
英伟达与其 GPU 客⼾之间的融资关系，正被摩根⼤通识别为 AI 信⽤压⼒潜在的传导路径。
以 CoreWeave 为代表的 GPU 运营商，依赖客⼾融资与信⽤⽀持维持 AI 基础设施的扩张。这⼀模
式在⽀撑增⻓的同时，也制造了⾼度相关的下⾏⻛险：⼀旦 GPU 运营商遭遇融资紧缩，压⼒将

沿供应链向上传导，最终反馈⾄英伟达等上游供应商。费城半导体指数（ SOX ）⽬前正测试 200
⽇均线⽀撑，为这⼀⻛险路径增添了重要的技术层⾯参照。
美国投资，亚洲受益 —— 也最先受损

### Page 4

AI 资本开⽀热潮在拉动美国国内需求的同时，⼤量资⾦正流向海外，形成不对称的外溢效应。
据 TS Lombard 数据，科技出⼝占 GDP ⽐重，韩国接近 20% 。这意味着，若 AI 资本开⽀出现⼤幅
收缩，亚洲出⼝经济体将⾸当其冲，承受不成⽐例的冲击；⽽美国国内经济实际获得的提振，
可能远⼩于表⾯的投资规模所暗⽰的⽔平。
AI 崩溃悖论：利率敏感板块或意外受益
TS Lombard 经济学家 Dario Perkins 提出了⼀个反直觉的逻辑： AI 投资热潮的终结，未必是全⾯
的经济灾难。
他认为，当前 AI 资本开⽀的急速扩张正在助推通胀，并使利率维持在更⾼⽔平，对房地产等利率
敏感板块形成持续挤压。若 AI 资本开⽀⼤幅回落，美联储将获得空间更为积极地降息，从⽽重新
激活⾮科技领域的需求，并对整体经济下⾏形成缓冲。换⾔之，下⼀轮 AI 热潮终结后的受益者，
恰恰可能是此前在这波浪潮中被边缘化的板块。
潜在买⼊窗⼝：条件尚未完全成熟
摩根⼤通对科技板块基本⾯维持建设性判断，但同时提⽰半导体定价可持续性不⾜及新兴云计

算（ neocloud ）融资压⼒等⻛险，软件板块相对韧性较强。

### Page 5

Document 219: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

Persistent energy pressures have further to run
19 October 2026
Key points
• Shipping disruptions, depleted
inventories and elevated freight
costs are keeping physical crude
and fuel markets tight despite a

reported recovery in Gulf exports.
• Brent is forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing
to US$77/bbl in Q4 2027.
• Refined product markets are
expected to ease more slowly than
crude, with elevated gasoil cracks,
shipping and insurance costs slowing
declines in petrol and diesel prices.
• Under an escalation scenario,
renewed conflict and deeper supply
disruptions could push Brent towards
US$150/bbl in H1 2027, prolonging
elevated fuel and LNG prices.

durable US-Iran agreement accelerates
supply recovery, taking Brent towards

fuel and LNG prices down faster.

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Low inventories and higher shipping costs will keep oil and
fuel prices elevated, with Brent futures forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027
as supply recovers. Refined fuels and LNG prices should

ease more slowly. Risks are two-sided: escalation could
push Brent towards US$150/bbl, while an early US-Iran
agreement could see a faster normalisation in supply with
prices returning close to pre-conflict levels by end-2027.
This article was first published in the October Market Outlook .
Brent crude is back above US$100/bbl on fading confidence
in a lasting US-Iran agreement and intermittent attacks on
shipping. Buffers remain depleted, with the IEA estimating

global observed inventories were 507mb lower at the end of
August than at the start of the conflict.
This comes despite reported crude exports through the Strait of
Hormuz recovering to around 70% to 90% of pre-conflict levels
in late September. Saudi and UAE exports through the Yanbu and
Fujairah pipelines continue, with the East-West pipeline back to
normal after the early September attacks. Tanker crossings have
risen, although transponders remain switched off, leaving visible

transit at 1% to 4% of pre-conflict levels, while cargo transfers in
the Gulf of Oman have increased as an alternative route.
However, these workarounds are slower and more costly. Ship-
to-ship transfers can take 5 to 10 days, US escorts through
the Gulf via a route close to the Oman shoreline cause delays
while some tankers are taking longer routes. Fewer ships are
also available, with many redeployed to other markets. Higher
insurance premiums are adding to costs, with Gulf-to-China

tanker rates above US$0.9mn a day, around 15x higher than a
year ago. With prompt barrels (available for physical delivery)
scarce, the gap between physical and futures prices widened
again, with Dated Brent around US$136/bbl in early October
against US$101/bbl for front-month futures.
Oil and fuel prices push higher ...
Refined prices have risen more than crude
Refined product markets were already tight going into the
conflict. Reduced Gulf supply, exacerbated by Ukrainian strikes

on Russian refineries, has added to that tightness. Together
with higher shipping costs and rising Dated Brent prices,
Singapore Gasoil 10ppm (diesel) has roughly doubled since the
Strait closed, compared with around 40% for crude futures.
Consequently, Singapore gasoil crack spread widened
to an average of around US$36.1/bbl in early October,
from US$17.5/bbl across 2025. Alongside the Australian
government’s excise tax returning to normal, after it had been

halved, this has lifted national diesel and petrol prices to
around A$2.83/L and A$2.41/L.
Outlook
We do not see a lasting US-Iran agreement until at least
mid-2027. In the meantime, we assume there will not be a
return to full-scale military intervention. However, periodic
Iranian attacks on ships and facilities are expected to continue,
as will the US blockade of Iranian exports, keeping a supply
risk premium in place. We look for Brent futures to average

around US$101/bbl in Q4, then ease gradually as demand
from refiners and governments to rebuild stocks partly offsets
recovering supply. As the premium fades and the market
moves into surplus in late 2027, we forecast Brent at around
US$77/bbl in Q4 2027, easing towards US$70/bbl in 2028 as
Middle East production fully recovers.
Oil balance to gradually improve over 2027
Global oil supply is expected to rise by 4.0mb/d (4.0%) to
103.7mb/d in 2027, following a 6.1% fall in 2026. Most of

this reflects a gradual recovery in Gulf output, with Houthi

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
attacks on Red Sea shipping keeping Saudi production below
its pre-conflict level until H2 2027. Meanwhile, we look for UAE
production to continue to improve over 2027 and lift above
pre-conflict levels from 2028, as it is no longer constrained by

OPEC quotas. We do not expect a meaningful return of Iranian
oil until an agreement is reached, and damage sustained during
the conflict means production will take time to recover.
We expect global oil demand to be 103.7mb/d in 2026, 0.8mb/d
(0.7%) higher than in 2025, and to rise a further 1.9mb/d (1.8%)
to 105.6mb/d in 2027. Chinese crude imports will continue to
recover over the coming months as refiners restock ahead of
Lunar New Year, but we expect only a gradual return to pre-

conflict levels, as the electrification of transport limits the
recovery in Chinese demand. Globally, demand for diesel and jet
fuel recovers as transport and aviation activity picks up, although
some demand destruction and efficiency gains are expected
to be permanent. The conflict has also highlighted strategic
vulnerabilities, particularly across Asia, so we expect a push to
rebuild reserves adding around 0.5% to demand over 2027.
Fuel prices to ease more slowly than crude

Refined product supply is expected to recover more slowly
than crude, with Gulf refinery runs not returning to pre-war
levels until at least late 2027. Outside the Gulf, there has
been some constructive news. The US has lifted sanctions
on Russian diesel until April 2027, with Russia saying it will
supply more than 4Mt over the coming months, while China
has lifted its ban on refined product exports. However, we
are cautious about how much this will add to the market in

the short term. Much of the Russian supply is headed to the
US and will depend on Russia’s ability to restore refining and
export capacity, while doubts remain over the Russia-Ukraine
energy ceasefire announced by President Trump. China still
manages exports through quotas, which are likely to increase
only gradually as domestic stocks are rebuilt. Refinery runs
should lift ahead of Lunar New Year, but any increase in
exports will take time to reach regional markets.

With product supply recovering slowly and depleted inventories
being rebuilt, Singapore gasoil crack is likely to stay above its
long-run average even as Brent eases, narrowing from US$71/
bbl in Q4 to US$27/bbl by Q4 2027. With Singapore Gas 10ppm
prices, shipping and insurance costs to remain elevated and
easing only gradually in 2027, we forecast Australian diesel
to retail at an average of 274¢/L in Q4, easing to 200¢/L in Q4
2027, and petrol at 230¢/L and 187¢/L respectively.

LNG prices supported through 2027
Japanese LNG prices surged 15.2%mth in September,
averaging US$25.4/MMBtu as supply disruptions in Qatar
and its inability to divert exports around the Strait of Hormuz
tightened markets and intensified competition between
importers. While prices are now more than double pre-conflict
levels, demand destruction across Asia is providing some
offset to tight supply
Our Q4 forecast for Japanese LNG is now US$24.5/mmbtu and

we expect the market to remain structurally tight, with supply
recovery likely to be gradual and prices not returning to pre-
conflict levels until around 2028. Still, Australia’s domestic
gas market should remain insulated, supported by the FY2027
Federal Budget’s domestic gas reservation scheme, which
requires exporters to supply the domestic market with the
equivalent of up to 20% of gas exports. So far this year,
despite the increase in LNG spot prices in Asia, Australian

natural gas prices have remained under the $12 per GJ cap
that has proposed by the Commonwealth government. We are
expecting this to continue well into 2027.
Risks around our oil price outlook are two-sided
Talks are still ongoing in the background, with the US and
Iran exchanging proposals to reopen the Strait, but the gap
between them remains wide. The conflict has also expanded
to the Red Sea and, with inventories sharply drawn down,
there is a smaller buffer to absorb a further shock.

Using the Oxford Economics model, we assess an escalation
scenario, where energy markets face a deeper and more
persistent disruption as the conflict in the Middle East
intensifies. In this case, Brent futures rise to around US$150/
bbl in H1 2027, only dipping to below US$100/bbl in Q2 2028.
We also consider an early resolution scenario, where shipping
and Gulf supply normalise faster, returning Brent to near pre-
conflict levels by end-2027.
Escalation scenario

In the escalation scenario we assume:
Talks stall and the conflict escalates:  the gap between the
US and Iran proves too wide and the US resumes strikes after
the midterms. Iran responds with attacks on shipping and Gulf
energy infrastructure, damaging export, storage and refining
facilities. A lasting agreement is not reached until 2028.
Export routes are disrupted: more frequent attacks curtail
dark crossings of the Strait and ship-to-ship transfers in the

Gulf of Oman. Houthi attacks also cut off Saudi alternative
routes, forcing Gulf producers to cut output and Middle
East crude exports sharply lower. New routes and shipping
arrangements eventually support a recovery in Gulf flows, but
trade patterns take time to adjust.

Product supply fails to recover:  The Russia–Ukraine energy
ceasefire does not hold and strikes on Russian refineries
continue, limiting how much diesel Russia can export,
even with the US sanctions waiver extended. China also

reintroduces its export ban to protect domestic supply.
Oil prices lift to around US$150/bbl in H1 2027: With little
buffer to absorb the supply loss, competition for physical
delivery intensifies, with physical prices well above futures
as prompt barrels remain scarce. Higher prices support
supply growth outside OPEC and drive demand destruction,
alongside further emergency stock releases, but a deeper
and more prolonged global deficit remains. Brent stays above

Early resolution scenario
In the early resolution scenario we assume:
A durable agreement: The US and Iran agree terms to reopen
the Strait by the end of year, and, unlike the June interim deal,

the agreement holds, with progress towards a permanent
settlement in early 2027. Attacks on shipping cease and the
US blockade is lifted, allowing Iranian oil back into the market,
initially from storage. Vessels are quickly repositioned to the
Gulf, with freight and war-risk insurance easing from early
2027 rather than mid-year.
A faster recovery in supply:  With export routes secure, Gulf
production and damaged refinery and export capacity recover

sooner than in the baseline. Outside the Gulf, the US extends
its waiver on Russian diesel beyond April 2027 and China
raises export quotas as domestic stocks are rebuilt.
Demand settles below pre-conflict trend: Some demand
destruction and efficiency gains persist, particularly in China.
With inventories rebuilding faster, government restocking is
smaller and more gradual than in the baseline.
Prices fall quickly, but restocking provides a floor:  Brent

Brent futures ease to around US$88/bbl in Q1 2027 as the
premium for prompt physical barrels unwinds. As the market
moves into balance, Brent eases to around US$65/bbl by Q4
2027, close to pre-conflict levels.
Refined product prices ease faster on lower freight costs with
the Singapore gasoil crack narrowing. Australian diesel and
petrol prices fall to around 182 cpl and 170 cpl by Q4 2027. Gas
markets also ease, although damage to Qatar’s gas facility means

export capacity recovers gradually. Japanese LNG falls to around
US$11.9/MMBtu by end-2027, $2.1 below our baseline.
Economic impact
In the escalation scenario, higher fuel costs flow through to
transport, food and other energy-intensive goods such as
plastics and fertilisers. Australian headline CPI remains above
4.5% for much of 2027, with underlying inflation remaining
sticky at above 3% until late 2028. The RBA faces a difficult
trade off between much higher inflation and a significant

hit to activity, as households cut discretionary spending and
businesses defer investment. Higher LNG export earnings
provide only a moderate offset. In the early resolution scenario,
headline inflation is around 0.1ppt below the baseline in 2027.
New Zealand is more exposed in both scenarios, with food
and energy a larger share of its CPI and no offsetting energy
exports. Headline inflation is around 1ppt higher in the
escalation scenario in Q1 2027.

U.S. macroeconomic performance has remained solid since the onset of the shock, as illustrated in particular by Q2 growth
of +3.8% and +9% (annualized quarterly rate) in consumer spending and private nonresidential investment, respectively.

In the manufacturing sector, business sentiment has gained momentum despite the shock, while the impact of this shock
on the services sector has remained very limited. The immediate reaction of consumer confidence was, however, more
negative before beginning to improve, which was nevertheless interrupted in August (latest available data for households’
sentiment on their future personal financial situation). The University of Michigan’s composite indicator continued to

deteriorate in September, due to the sharp rise in fuel prices. But declining confidence still does not translate into reduced
spending—at least not at the aggregate level.
On the inflation front, the spike immediately following the outbreak of the conflict in Iran was more pronounced than in
the months following Russia’s invasion of Ukraine. The decline in June still left inflation at a high level, but its stability in
July and August was notable (3.4% y/y), as was the latest—the third consecutive—slight decline in inflation excluding

energy in August (2.5% y/y, same rate as in February). Given the existing inflationary pressures, however, inflation is highly
likely to resume its upward trend in September.

Document 383: ‘Cost-Plus’ Costs (2026-10-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/‘Cost-Plus’ Costs (2026-10-27).pdf

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #labour #rba #productivity
‘Cost-Plus’ Costs
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
10:40 October 16 2026 Share
Entrenched expectations that cost increases can and should be passed on are precisely
what tight monetary policy is leaning against, but only indirectly.
The recent tendency of some firms to bundle into prices card-acceptance costs that had previously
been surcharged – and then some, in some cases – demonstrates a ‘cost-plus’ approach to pricing.

### Page 2

The recent banning of card surcharges has caused considerable upset this month, even though it is a
reversion to historical and international practice. The card schemes used to impose “no-surcharge”
rules, until the RBA banned these in 2003, and many other countries still have them. The turnaround
represents the RBA throwing in the towel on its longstanding effort to use price incentives to get people
to choose low-cost payment methods. Surcharges no longer made sense in a largely cashless
economy. In any case, least-cost routing puts the power to choose lower-cost methods in the hands of
the merchants who actually bear the cost.

In most cases, firms will simply roll the surcharge into the sticker price, leaving the total cost to the
consumer unchanged, just more transparent. We also understand that some large retailers have
absorbed the cost – if they were surcharging in the first place, which many did not. (The RBA estimated
that around 16% of businesses imposed surcharges.) Inevitably, though, some firms have lifted their
prices by more than any reasonable estimate of the merchant service fee that the surcharge was
meant to cover. And because this is Australia, of course takeaway coffee has been the centre of
consumer discontent.

Higher prices for one’s caffeine fix was unlikely to be the main instigator of this month’s slump in
consumer sentiment. Petrol prices are typically more salient for cost-of-living concerns, and the before-
and-after picture on the RBA announcement that week shows that higher interest rates also contributed
to the decline.

In some senses, though, the post-surcharge prices and the RBA’s decisions are linked: businesses
passing on increased costs into their own prices – and expecting to be able to make that increase stick
– is exactly the kind of inflationary environment the RBA is leaning against. Firms have been subjected
to an array of non-labour cost escalations, from pandemic-era supply chain issues to electricity to fuel
to water to building supplies. And IT equipment prices, which for decades were a reliable source of cost
reductions, are nowadays flat to up. Passing these costs on, leaving margins intact, demonstrates that
demand is not weak enough to prevent this, and signals that firms’ inflationary expectations might be
higher than the RBA desires. The RBA will take this as a sign that demand is still outstripping supply.

To the extent they can pass on these costs through to prices, it suggests that demand was strong
enough to bear it. The RBA will take this as a signal that demand is still outstripping supply.
This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. Base wages growth has been moderate for some
years, though the above-average increase in award wages this year will add a little. Not all firms will
seek to pass these cost increases on, but some will, including because they have limited scope to
absorb the cost increase into margins.
A cost-plus mentality has drawbacks, including that it limits the imagination away from finding
alternative, efficiency-improving responses. It also accords with recent academic research
suggesting that firms form expectations and set prices according to what is happening in their own
industry and experience, while putting little weight on economy-wide inflation or central bank
credibility. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-sensitive
sector when supply shocks abound.

### Page 3

This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. While the Fair Work Commission did hand down an
above-average increase in award wages this year and this was probably passed through to prices in
hospitality and other labour-intensive sectors, base wages growth has been moderate for some years.
Business surveys point to non-labour cost escalation being the real issue lately. This is why we were
puzzled by the passage in the RBA minutes this week suggesting that cost pressures reported in
business surveys might imply that the unemployment rate consistent with inflation at target (the NAIRU)
has risen. It is a convenient belief, though, if unemployment is starting to overshoot your forecasts, as
the latest labour market data have done compared with the RBA’s August forecasts.

Not all firms can pass on cost increases. Most small firms do not have the market power, though coffee
may be an exception because convenience induces a kind of local market power. And as noted above,
large firms with everyday-low-price strategies will instead try to find savings elsewhere in the supply
chain.

Still, some firms will seek to pass these cost increases on. Some observers might see this as corporate
greed, but another way to frame this strategy is simply as a ‘cost-plus’ mentality. It turns out that cost-
plus pricing is especially common in areas where regulators determine the price – water and other
utilities being examples where costs and prices have escalated a lot in recent years.

There are limits to firms’ ability to absorb costs, too. The failures of so many building firms a few years
ago, squeezed between post-pandemic cost escalation, delivery delays and fixed-price contracts, were
a case in point. Normally, there is a contractual response, and it need not end in insolvency. The
episode does illustrate, however, that it is unrealistic to expect firms never to pass cost increases
through to prices.

The cost-plus mentality does have its drawbacks. It limits the imagination away from finding responses
that might save costs elsewhere to absorb the cost hike, and in so doing, boost efficiency. It was
equally limiting in times of soft demand: in the years before the pandemic, many firms professed a
belief that, because they couldn’t get price increases to stick, they could not possibly accept cost
increases, including in wages. Thus, Australia ended up with wages growth too low to be consistent
with inflation at target, and the peculiar spectacle of a central bank governor complaining that wages
growth was too low. And if labour remains cheap, why invest to economise on it and thereby boost
productivity?

A cost-plus mentality also amplifies the pass-through of supply shocks. This is not quite the same thing
as the ‘inflation expectations’ seen in standard economic models of inflation, but it is related. The
stronger the tendency towards cost-plus pricing, the worse the inflation response to a supply shock.
This is also true when inflation expectations are high, but the distinction matters. The recent academic
literature includes some fascinating empirical evidence from New Zealand that suggests that beliefs
about current and future overall inflation have little bearing on many firms’ pricing decisions. As related
work using French data shows, firms’ pricing choices are shaped less by economy-wide inflation
developments than by what is happening to their costs and in their industry.

### Page 4

Related articles
That adds up to economy-wide behaviour that looks a lot like firms (in aggregate) setting prices
according to expected inflation (in aggregate), but under the hood something quite different is going on.
If firms’ beliefs about future economy-wide inflation do not matter much in their pricing decisions, then
neither does the credibility of a central bank’s commitment to return inflation to target. The mechanism
to get inflation down then must be simply to weaken demand enough to convince firms that cost-plus
will not fly in the market. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-
sensitive sector when supply shocks abound. It does, however, imply that if supply shocks fade or even
self-correct, so will high inflation. If and when that happens, tight monetary policy can be unwound.

The deeper question is whether cost-plus price dynamics can be reduced. That is more likely to be a
question for regulators than for central banks.

帶動工業產業預估本益比達到近30年的高點27倍。
雖然近期工業受到高油價與國防政策利空已經回檔
7%，但9/22預估本益比仍高達 22.6倍，高於過去
五年平均20.9倍，也高於整體標普500的19.1倍。

重點摘要
• 油價與運價維持高檔 ，航空
產業需求遭遇逆風

壓力
《油價與運價維持高檔，航空產業需求遭遇逆風》
近期受到中東衝突升溫導致僅有少量油輪通過荷姆
茲海峽，貨櫃船實際上仍無法通行。過去5-6年全球
港口吞吐運量成長遠低於船舶訂單量成長 ，導致重
大港口的貨櫃塞港嚴重 。中東地緣政治風險和全球
性的港口擁擠預估短期內都未能改善契機 ，因此未
來半年貨櫃運價仍將維持高位 。由於過去半年美國
機票價格已大幅上漲 ，導致價格敏感的美國旅客消
費開始偏向保守。美國運輸安全管理局(簡稱TSA)統
計每日旅客吞吐量近期呈現持續下滑 ，顯示航空業
持續性漲價已經衝擊到旅客需求 。隨著機構上調

4Q26原油預測 10%到88美元，高價位的航空燃油
價格也將再次對全球航空公司運量與獲利造成衝擊 。
《眾議院通過限制擴大對伊朗軍事行動 ，對國防類
股形成壓力》
今年上半年因中東衝突升溫及美伊緊張情勢 ，國防
股曾出現一波強勁上漲 。但隨著美國眾議院 9/15以
220票對204票通過限制總統對伊朗軍事行動的決議
案，要求未獲國會授權前不得持續或擴大對伊朗的
軍事行動 。雖然美國國會決議案主要是限制新增的
軍事行動授權 ，並非削減國防預算 。但美國國會限
制進一步軍事行動 ，已經造成市場資金對國防類股
進行獲利了結 。考量航空類股仍將面臨高油價逆風 ，
以及美國眾議院擴大限制政府對伊朗軍事行動 。本
行對工業產業展望維持中立評等。
資料來源：TSA、JP Morgan，2026/9/17，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/24

Document 487: 从“囚徒困境”到“反身性”—主宰AI交易的市场博弈

Source PDF: all report pdf/从“囚徒困境”到“反身性”—主宰AI交易的市场博弈.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

AI 巨头陷⼊了⼀个谁都不敢退出的囚徒困境：停投可能落后，⻬投⼜会推⾼供给、压缩回
报。更⼤的变化在于， AI 建设正从经营现⾦流驱动转向融资驱动，市场信⼼开始直接影响
资本开⽀规模，这便是 “ 反⾝性 ” ⻛险。市场需要盯住的，已不只是 AI 能⼒和需求，还有收⼊
兑现速度能否⽀撑这场⾼强度融资竞赛。
摘要：
上周， AI 基础设施的两条⻛险线索同时浮出⽔⾯： CoreWeave 遭遇股债双杀，融资成本急升；
Meta 的巨额 AI 资本开⽀和⾃由现⾦流转负则再次引发市场对投⼊产出⽐的担忧。前者暴露了⾼
杠杆基建公司的融资脆弱性，后者让投资者重新审视科技巨头不断加码的资本开⽀。
这不只是两家公司⾯临的压⼒，更指向同⼀个问题：当 AI 建设越来越依赖外部融资，这场军备
竞赛还能持续多久？
云计算巨头们深陷⼀个经典的囚徒困境 —— 谁都清楚集体过度投资的代价，却没有任何⼀家敢
于单⽅⾯踩刹⻋。据路透社分析 LSEG 共识预测数据，微软、 Alphabet 、亚⻢逊、 Meta 和甲⻣⽂
五家超⼤规模云⼚商，预计到 2027 年资本开⽀总额将超过其⾃由现⾦流，每新增 1 美元运营现⾦

流，对应约 1.57 美元的新增资本开⽀。这⼀剪⼑差，正在将曾经以 " 轻资产 " 著称的科技平台，推
向⼀种对资本市场⾼度依赖的新商业模式。
投资⼈ Citrini 在接受 Monetary Matters 播客采访时直接点出了这⼀转变的核⼼⻛险： " 在这场 AI 交
易的前三年，情绪驱动的下跌都有⼀个底 —— 因为⼤部分⽀出来⾃超⼤规模云⼚商的运营现⾦
流。但现在，越来越多的基础设施是靠着相当惊⼈的预期来融资的。 " 当融资来源从内部现⾦流
转向股权发⾏和债务融资，市场情绪本⾝就获得了直接影响实体投资规模的能⼒。
这正是 " 反⾝性（ Reflexivity ，指的是原因与结果之间存在循环的、双向的相互作⽤与影响）" ⻛
险：信⼼⽀撑融资，融资⽀撑投资；⼀旦信⼼转弱，资⾦收缩⼜会反过来打击投资与增⻓预
期，形成负向循环。
AI 正在重塑科技巨头，也正在重塑科技巨头⾃⾝的商业模式。驱动这场 AI 基建军备竞赛的逻辑或
许已从" 投资推动增⻓ " 悄然滑向 " 信⼼决定投资 " 。
没有⼈敢停下来：囚徒困境的博弈结构

Cornell Capital Group 的研究报告《 AI 囚徒困境》将这场军备竞赛的内在逻辑描述得相当清晰：
任何⼀家巨头，如果在 AI 上重注⽽竞争对⼿没有，就能获得市场主导权；如果⾃⼰不投⽽对⼿
投了，则⾯临被淘汰的⻛险。因此，⽆论竞争对⼿如何选择，激进投资都是每家公司的 " 理性策
略 " 。
数字印证了这⼀逻辑的烈度。 2024 年，亚⻢逊、 Alphabet 、微软、 Meta 和苹果五家公司合计资
本开⽀约 2270 亿美元； 2025 年这⼀数字跃升⾄ 3910 亿美元；管理层对 2026 年的指引显⽰总额
可能突破 6350 亿美元。⾼盛预测， 2025 年⾄ 2027 年间，超⼤规模云⼚商的累计资本开⽀将达
1.15 万亿⾄ 1.4 万亿美元 —— 是 2022 年⾄ 2024 年 4770 亿美元的逾两倍。
如果市场情绪急转直下，那些增量资⾦就会枯竭 ——因为这不再是⼀家公司花⾃⼰的钱，⽽是由
市场决定的。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

具体到各家公司， 2026 年的指引更为激进：亚⻢逊⽬标⽀出 2000 亿美元， Alphabet 指引 1750 亿
⾄ 1850 亿美元，其 CEO Sundar Pichai 甚⾄表⽰ " 这个数字可能还不够 " ， Meta 指引最⾼ 1350 亿
美元。
分析师 Amin Mrini 在其报告《 7000 亿美元的囚徒困境》中指出，这五家公司合计承诺的 2026 年
资本开⽀约为其合并⾃由现⾦流的 3.8 倍 ——" 历史上最能产⽣现⾦的企业，正⾛在每产⽣ 1 美元
⾃由现⾦流就要花掉近 4 美元的轨道上，靠着创纪录的债务发⾏来填补缺⼝。 "
这⼀困境的结构性根源在于基础设施的不可压缩性。数据中⼼、电⼒协议、专⽤芯⽚，全部需
要多年的提前采购和建设周期。等到需求在收⼊端清晰可⻅，响应窗⼝早已关闭。 Mrini 写
道： " 正是这种滞后，将预期变成了义务。 "
历史的镜⼦：铁路、航空与光纤的宿命
Cornell Capital 的报告援引了三段历史，为这场 AI 军备竞赛提供了令⼈警醒的参照系。

19 世纪美国铁路热潮中，铁路股票和债券总额达 106 亿美元（约合今⽇ 4000 亿美元），远超当时
12 亿美元的国债规模。铁路确实改变了美国，但 1873 年恐慌后的 " ⻓期萧条 " 中，九成铁路公司
倒闭， 1893 年恐慌期间四分之⼀的铁路⾥程进⼊破产接管。受益者是制造商、农业⽣产者和商
⼈，⽽⾮铁路股东。

### Page 3

商业航空的故事更为直⽩。过去 80 年间，全球航空业累计净利润约 860 亿美元，⽽仅新冠疫情三
年就抹去了近 1900 亿美元。沃伦 · 巴菲特曾如此总结这⼀结构性困境： " 如果⼀位有远⻅的资本家
当年在⼩鹰镇在场，他最好的选择是把奥维尔 · 莱特打下来，这样能为后来的投资者省去⽆数⿇
烦。 " 巴菲特本⼈最终在 2020 年以约 50 亿美元的亏损清仓了四家美国航空公司的持仓。
1990 年代末的电信泡沫则是最直接的类⽐。彼时超过 5000 亿美元涌⼊光纤电缆和⽆线⽹络，⼤
部分靠债务融资。到 2002 年，超过 23 家主要电信公司申请破产，约 2 万亿美元市值灰⻜烟灭，不
到 5% 的已铺设光纤被点亮使⽤。然⽽，正是这些破产投资者留下的廉价带宽，孕育了 Netflix 、
YouTube 和整个云计算产业。
Cornell Capital 的结论⼀针⻅⾎： " 变⾰性技术的悖论在于：公司花得越多、竞争越激烈，技术
商品化就越快。商品化对消费者是福⾳，对投资者的利润率却是腐蚀剂。"

现⾦流的剪⼑差：数据层⾯的压⼒测试
路透社对 LSEG 数据的分析，为上述逻辑提供了实证⽀撑。
甲⻣⽂是⽬前压⼒最为显著的案例。其资本开⽀占运营现⾦流的⽐例，从 2022 财年的 47% 飙升
⾄ 2026 财年（截⾄今年 5 ⽉）的 174%—— 当年资本开⽀ 557 亿美元，运营现⾦流仅 320 亿美元，
⾃由现⾦流已转负。该公司股价今年以来已下跌 36% ，并计划通过债务和股权融资筹集 450 亿⾄
500 亿美元⽤于云基础设施扩张。
微软在最近⼀个财季录得 358 亿美元运营现⾦流，同期资本开⽀（含融资租赁）达 375 亿美元。
亚⻢逊过去 12 个⽉运营现⾦流同⽐增⻓ 30% ⾄ 1485 亿美元，但⾃由现⾦流已降⾄ 12 亿美元。

### Page 4

Futurum Equities ⾸席市场策略师 Shay Boloor 表⽰： " 投资者低估了 AI 正在从根本上改变⼤型科
技公司商业模式的程度。这些公司历史上被视为轻资产平台，因为收⼊增速可以远超资本需
求，但 AI 正在将它们推向⼀种混合模式，软件、⼴告和云计算的经济性越来越依赖于庞⼤的实体
基础设施⽀出。 "
Mrini 的测算更为严峻：按当前指引速度推算资本开⽀⾄ 2027 年，再以 15% 的税前投资回报率为
基准， AI 计算收⼊需要在 2030 年达到约 2.9 万亿美元，才能覆盖已部署资本的成本 —— 这相当于
当前整个云计算市场规模的三倍以上。从当前约 2110 亿美元的基数出发，这意味着需要连续五
年维持约 68% 的年增⻓率， " 在基础设施市场中没有任何先例 " 。
反⾝性：当融资结构改变了⻛险性质
Citrini 在访谈中对这⼀转变的描述，是理解当前 AI 交易⻛险最为精准的框架之⼀。
他将 AI 投资的融资结构变化分为两个阶段。第⼀阶段，超⼤规模云⼚商⽤⾃⾝运营现⾦流⽀撑⽀

出，这意味着⽆论市场情绪如何波动，只要这些公司持续投⼊，半导体等受益公司的基本⾯就
有⼀个 " 底 " 。" ⽆论是关税、⽇元套利交易，还是我都记不清 2023 年 9 ⽉我们在担⼼什么，基本
⾯仍然在改善。 "
第⼆阶段，也就是当下，情况已经根本不同。Citrini 直接说： " 现在， AI 领域每⼀笔增量⽀出，⼏
乎都来⾃股权融资 —— ⽆论是⻛险投资还是公开市场 —— 以及债务融资。这意味着， AI 可以变得
更有⽤，使⽤量可以持续扩张，但你可能为供应它的算⼒付出了过⾼的代价；或者，情绪可以
产⽣更为显著的冲击 —— 因为不像以前有基本⾯托底，如果市场情绪转向，那些增量资⾦就会
枯竭，因为这不再只是⼀家公司在花钱，⽽是由市场决定的。 "

### Page 5

这⼀机制的核⼼在于：融资来源的变化，使得市场情绪从⼀个外⽣变量，变成了⼀个可以直接
影响投资规模的内⽣变量。 Citrini 将这⼀动态称为 " 反⾝性 "—— 信⼼⽀撑融资，融资⽀撑投资，
投资⽀撑收⼊预期，收⼊预期反过来⽀撑信⼼。⽽⼀旦这条链条上的任何⼀环出现裂缝，整个
正反馈就可能逆转。
他给出了⼀个具体的触发情景： " 我认为这个周期的转折，会以⼀声叹息⽽⾮⼀声爆炸的⽅式到
来。不会是某家公司违约引发崩盘，⽽是：我们把实验室的合并 ARR 预测从 4000 亿美元下调到
3600 亿美元，然后因为这种增⻓已经被充分定价，这个增量变化就会向外扩散，把整个体系拉
低。 "
捷⽂斯悖论失效：需求弹性的关键缺⼝
⽀撑 AI 基础设施投资的⼀个核⼼乐观假设，是 " 捷⽂斯悖论 " （ Jevons Paradox ） —— 算⼒成本下
降会刺激需求爆发，从⽽吸收所有新增产能。 Mrini 在报告中承认这⼀逻辑的存在，但同时指出
其在 AI 语境下的局限：企业集成缓慢、监管约束真实存在，⽽效率提升主要发⽣在模型层，需求

响应可能流向应⽤开发者和模型提供商，⽽⾮销售算⼒的基础设施层。 " 捷⽂斯悖论可以在 AI 作
为技术层⾯成⽴，同时在 AI 基础设施作为商业模式层⾯失效。 "
Citrini 援引了 Anthropic 发布的经济指数数据：任务成本每降低 10% ，使⽤量仅增加约 3% 。也许
你在模型不好⽤的时候， token 需求反⽽更多，因为你得⼀直修改。 " 这根本不是捷⽂斯悖
论。 " 他还指出，当前 AI 收⼊增速约为每年 25% ⾄ 30% ，⽽资本开⽀增速接近 60% ⾄ 70% ，两者
之间的缺⼝随每⼀轮指引周期⽽扩⼤。
他对 " 什么时候会重新看多半导体 " 给出了明确条件： " 如果我们看到付费使⽤量持续增⻓，第⼆
阶段导数重新加速，并且能够真正通过客⼾现⾦流来⽀撑融资 —— 这需要采⽤率真正提升，尤
其是对最前沿模型的采⽤。 "
市场已经开始区分：谁在卖算⼒，谁在⽤ AI 赚钱
同样是⾼额 AI 资本开⽀，市场对不同公司的反应已经出现分化。
Mrini 观察到， Meta 获得市场认可的原因，是其 AI 投⼊已经体现在⼴告定向和⼴告效果衡量的改

善上，带来收⼊增⻓和利润率提升。算⼒在这⾥嵌⼊了具有定价能⼒的业务，效率提升能够直
接降低服务成本。

### Page 6

相⽐之下， Google 和微软仍处于投⼊更清晰、应⽤层回报尚未完全反映在利润表中的阶段。两
家公司都在将 AI 嵌⼊搜索、办公软件、 Android 、企业协作和智能代理⼯具中，也都在争夺智能
代理⼯作流的编排和协调⼊⼝。但市场短期看到的，⾸先仍是数据中⼼账单。
亚⻢逊和甲⻣⽂的处境则更接近基础设施提供者。亚⻢逊虽然有 Alexa+ 和⾃研 Trainium 等布
局，但⼤部分投⼊仍指向按⼩时出售的 AWS 算⼒；甲⻣⽂对云基础设施的依赖更⾼，融资压⼒
也更显著。
差别在于， AI 投⼊能否转化为具备定价权的产品收⼊，还是主要依赖出售标准化计算资源。
这也是 AI 交易下⼀阶段最关键的分界线：资本开⽀本⾝不再⾜以构成利好，市场需要看到收⼊、
利润率和现⾦流之间更明确的传导。
路透社援引 Winthrop Capital Management ⾼级交易员 Freddy Lavric 的话称： " 未来两到三年，
公司需要证明 AI 正在带来增量收⼊、扩⼤利润率并改善现⾦流。如果这些财务收益没有显现，市

场将开始质疑这⼀轮投资是否⾛得太远。 "
AI 交易的真正考验，是资本能否熬到回报兑现
AI 带来的技术进步并没有消失，需求也没有凭空蒸发。超⼤规模云⼚商继续扩⼤基础设施投⼊，
恰恰说明它们相信 AI 将成为⻓期竞争⼒的核⼼。
问题在于，所有参与者都认为⾃⼰不能缺席，便可能共同制造超额供给；所有参与者都提⾼融
资强度，市场情绪便可能从旁观者变成决定投资规模的变量。
囚徒困境解释了巨头为何持续加码：谁都害怕落后。
反⾝性则解释了市场为何变得脆弱：当内部现⾦流⽆法覆盖新增投⼊，股价、债券、融资意愿
与资本开⽀会开始相互影响。乐观时，这套机制能放⼤扩张；⼀旦收⼊兑现速度低于预期，它
同样会放⼤收缩。
AI 基础设施最终会成为⾼回报资产，还是⼀台昂贵的商品化跑步机，取决于⼀个朴素却极难回答
的问题：需求增⻓能否跑赢算⼒价格下降，以及这种答案能否在今天投⼊的设备完成折旧之前

### Page 7

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 1

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  金融‧美國

Bank of America Corporation 
(BAC.N/BAC.US)

3Q26獲利受一次性罰款影響; 2027F核心獲利維持動能 
重要訊息 
美國銀行 (BAC) 3Q26 每股盈餘 0.91 元，低於市場預估 17%，若扣除反洗錢
罰款 12億美元(每股盈餘 0.17元)，則低於市場預估 3%。 
評論及分析 
3Q26 獲利衰退受一次性罰款及資本市場 波動影響。BAC 3Q26 稅後淨利僅
69.3 億美元，季減 24%、年減 17%，若扣除 12 億美元之一次性反洗錢監
管罰款，調整後淨利為 81 億美元，季減 10%、年減 2%，主要非利息收入
季減 13%(投資收益季減 44%、手續費收入季減 3%)，反映 3Q26 資本市場
波動，抵銷核心淨利息收入(NII)季增 3%。3Q26 淨呆帳提存季減 7%，抵銷
營業費用季增 6%(扣除罰款則季減 7%)。1-3Q26 營運槓桿因一次性罰款則
轉為負向 0.45 百分點 ，若扣除一次性費用則仍為正 1.8 百分點。  
3Q26 淨利差持續上揚、資產品質持續改善 。BAC 3Q26 淨利差季增 8 基點
至 2.16%，主要來自 3Q26 放款年增 6%、季增 1% (主要美國商業放款及
信用卡各年增 9%及 5%)、低收益資產到期 重新訂價，帶動存放利差擴大 5
基點。3Q26 總存款年增 2%、季增 1%，資金成本因存款結構持續優化( 非
計息存款年增  5%)，季增幅僅 提升 3 基點至  1.49%。資產品質 亦持續改
善，3Q26 呆帳沖銷率季減 3 基點至 0.44%，主要信用卡呆帳沖銷率季 減
33 基點至 3.22%，企金沖銷率季減 1 基點，帶動 3Q26 逾放比季減 0.03%
至 0.44%。BAC 3Q26 普通股權益比率(CET1)季減 35 基點至 10.9%，其中
AOCI 之債券部位虧損 18 億元(影響約 10 基點)，但資本返還超過獲利，配
發股利及實施庫藏股共 83 億元，高於主要同業。  
2027F 淨利息收入展望正向，營運槓桿 回升至 200-300 基點。管理層重申
2026F 淨利息收入達年增 8%目標， 並預期 2027 淨利息收入年增超過
8%(約增加 51 億美元)，主要反映非計息存款成長、放款成長、固定利率重
定價及 3 次升息 25 基點之效益，中期淨利差目標維持 2.3%。在營運效率
方面，公司預期 4Q26 回到正向營運槓桿，2027F 營運槓桿目標 200-300 基
點。針對市場關注的監管缺失，管理層表示已取得重大改善進展，並強調本
次同意令與富國銀行過往情況不同，並未設有資產規模上限。公司對 2027F
資產品質有信心，指出目前未見轉弱跡象。 至於 AI 授信與風險控管政策，
BAC 強調嚴格評估借款人的現金流集中度、 企業現金流。  
投資建議 
美國銀行雖一次性罰款影響今年獲利約 4%，但核心營運動能穩定，隨著存
放款結構持續優化，公司預估 2027F 淨利息收入年增率超過 8%，加上資產
品質良好，2027F 營運槓桿回升至 200-300 基點，公司維持穩定股利配發及
買回庫藏股，維持美國銀行「增加持股」評等 ，目標價 67 元，約 2026F -
27F 平均每股盈餘 14 倍(過去五年本益比區間為 8-16 倍)。 
投資風險 
通膨造成美國聯準會再度升息；美國 消費支出趨緩；全球金融市場轉弱 。

### Page 5

美國 Bank of America Corporation

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

圖 14：公司概況  圖 15：BAC 組織架構圖 
美國銀行 (Bank of America Corporation) 創立於 1784 年，總
部位於美國北卡羅來納州夏洛特，為美國第二大銀行。截至
2Q25 總資產規模為 3.44 兆美元，員工總數約 21.2 萬人。公
司透過八大事業體營運，涵蓋消費金融、優選銀行、商業銀
行、企業與投資銀行、財富管理與全球市場業務，客戶遍及全
球逾 30 個國家，服務近 6,900 萬名個人與中小企業客戶。 
美國銀行積極發展數位化與國際布局，強調資本效率與風險控
管，並透過穩定配息與股份回購持續強化股東回饋，品牌形象
聚焦穩健經營、數位創新與永續金融發展。

### Page 7

美國 Bank of America Corporation

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量 (十億焦耳 ) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量  = 直接能源消耗總量  + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電 /電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力
（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下  
購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量 (十億焦耳 ) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量   
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源  
再生能源使用率  再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量 (公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍  1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳  (CO2)、甲烷  (CH4)、一氧化二氮  (N2O)、氫氟碳化物  (HFCS)、全氟化合物  (PFCS)、六氟化硫  (SF6)、三氟
化氮 (NF3)  
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍  1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳  (CO2)、甲烷  (CH4)、一氧化二氮  (N2O)、氫氟碳化物  (HFCS)、全氟化合物  (PFCS)、六氟化硫  (SF6)、三氟
化氮 (NF3)  
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量 (公噸) 
- 總廢棄物  = 無害廢棄物  + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業， tailings 、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內  
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率  = 廢棄物回收量 /總廢棄物 *100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物  
總取水量 
總取水量 (立方公尺 ) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮 /公用事業 /市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮  
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出  
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職 /死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、
裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率  =（離開之員工 /平均員工人數） *100 
- 平均員工人數  =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數） /2 
- 本財年末員工數  =上個財年末員工數  + 新員工數  - 離開之員工數  
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比  = 女性經理人數 /經理總數 *100  
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比  = 女性人數 /員工總數 *100  
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以  8，係假設  1 天 =  8 小時工作  
每位員工教育訓練時數  平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
股東治理分數  衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性  
公司治理分數  衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性  
產品責任分數  衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量  
社區關係分數  衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾  
勞動力分數  衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效  
資源使用指標  衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案
